Method: 采用前几何引力理论框架研究具有球对称性的黑洞构型。通过对应原理，求解不同前几何理论在真空中的精确解。这些解是经典(反)德西特-施瓦西黑洞度规在前几何框架下的类似物。同时对前几何版本的比尔科夫定理进行理论分析，以验证解的性质和唯一性。

Result: 成功获得了多个前几何理论下的球对称黑洞真空精确解，这些解是(反)德西特-施瓦西度规的前几何对应物。特别地，其中一个前几何解展现出普朗克尺度大小的(反)德西特核心结构。完成了前几何版本比尔科夫定理的分析，为解的唯一性提供了理论支持。

Conclusion: 前几何引力理论能够为黑洞提供非奇异的描述，通过自发对称性破缺机制，经典的(反)德西特-施瓦西黑洞几何可以从前几何配置中涌现出来。前几何黑洞解中出现的普朗克尺度核心结构为解决黑洞奇点问题提供了新途径。前几何版本的比尔科夫定理确保了球对称真空解的理论自洽性，这为进一步研究量子引力和黑洞物理奠定了基础。

Abstract: Pre-geometric Gravity investigates the non-metric features of spacetime near the Planck scale, from which the gravitational interaction arises as a low-energy effective theory via a mechanism of spontaneous symmetry breaking. With a view of amending the classical singularities found in black hole geometries, in this work we study pre-geometric black hole configurations with spherical symmetry, and present exact solutions for different pre-geometric theories in vacuum via the correspondence principle. These solutions are the pre-geometric analogue of the (anti-)de Sitter-Schwarzschild metric, from which such type of black hole geometry is thus expected to emerge. In one case, the pre-geometric solution also exhibits an (anti-)de Sitter core of Planckian size. The main result is supplemented with an analysis of the pre-geometric version of Birkhoff's theorem.

</details>


### [127] [Probing hairy Kerr black holes through quasi-periodic oscillations II: a study based on the resonance models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11378)
*Anirban Dasgupta,Supragyan Priyadarshinee,Indrani Banerjee,Subhash Mahapatra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了引力解耦框架下构建的旋转毛发黑洞，通过九种共振模型结合先前的两种运动学模型，对六个黑洞源的高频准周期振荡(HFQPO)观测数据进行系统分析，发现当前精度的HFQPO数据无法排除毛发克尔黑洞场景，并为评估不同HFQPO模型的性能提供了系统框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准克尔黑洞由质量、电荷和自旋完全描述，但毛发黑洞引入了额外的标量场或物质场自由度，为检验克尔范式的偏离提供了新框架。高频准周期振荡(HFQPO)观测数据为探测黑洞毛发提供了潜在途径，但需要系统比较不同理论模型与观测数据的符合程度，以评估毛发黑洞的可行性。

Method: 研究采用引力解耦框架构建满足爱因斯坦场方程和强能量条件的旋转毛发黑洞时空。针对六个黑洞源的HFQPO观测数据，系统测试了九种共振模型，并结合先前工作(Paper I)中的两种运动学模型，共计十一种模型进行比较分析。采用赤池信息准则(AIC)评估模型性能，并检验推断自旋与先前独立估计的一致性，建立了系统的模型评估框架。

Result: 基于两种运动学模型和九种共振模型的综合分析表明，在当前观测精度下，HFQPO数据无法排除毛发克尔黑洞场景。通过AIC准则和自旋一致性检验，系统评估了不同HFQPO模型对各个源的相对表现。分析揭示了不同模型在拟合观测数据方面的差异，为每个黑洞源提供了模型性能的定量比较。

Conclusion: 研究结果突出了高频准周期振荡作为探测黑洞毛发工具的潜力，但当前观测精度尚不足以对毛发克尔黑洞场景做出明确判断。需要更高精度的观测数据和对准周期振荡物理起源的更深入理解，才能有效区分标准克尔黑洞与毛发黑洞。本文建立的系统评估框架为未来利用HFQPO数据检验黑洞时空性质提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Hairy black holes (BHs), characterized by nontrivial scalar or matter fields, introduce additional degrees of freedom beyond mass, charge, and spin, providing a framework for testing deviations from the Kerr paradigm. We investigate a class of rotating hairy black holes constructed within the gravitational decoupling framework, whose spacetime satisfies the Einstein field equations with a conserved energy-momentum tensor consistent with the strong energy conditions. In our previous work (Paper I; Dasgupta et al. 2026), we studied their horizon structure and test-particle dynamics and confronted two kinematic high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillation (HFQPO) models with observations of six black hole sources. Building on that analysis, we here test nine resonance models against the same HFQPO data, enabling a systematic comparison of eleven models (two kinematic and nine resonance) within the hairy Kerr framework. A comprehensive analysis based on the two kinematic models (Paper I) and the nine resonance models reveals that the HFQPO data with the current level of precision cannot rule out the hairy Kerr scenario. By implementing the Akaike Information Criterion and by examining the consistency of the inferred spins with previous independent spin estimates, our analysis provides a systematic framework for assessing the relative performance of different HFQPO models for each source. These results highlight the potential of HFQPOs to probe black hole hair and the need for more precise observations and a better understanding of the physical origin of QPOs.

</details>


### [128] [Probing Kerr spacetime via interferometric signatures of higher-order images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11391)
*Fabiano Feleppa,Fabio Aratore,Valerio Bozza,Lorenzo Albanese*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对慢旋转Kerr黑洞时空中的紧凑源,建立了高阶图像干涉测量特征的解析描述框架,揭示了黑洞自旋和源动力学在长基线可见度模式中留下的特征印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞不稳定光子区附近的光子轨道产生的高阶图像编码了时空几何信息,可通过长基线干涉测量观测。然而,缺乏对这些特征的系统性解析描述,特别是如何从干涉测量信号中识别黑洞自旋和源动力学效应的理论框架尚不完善。

Method: 采用强偏转极限近似,推导了慢旋转Kerr黑洞时空中有限尺寸源的复可见度函数(在黑洞自旋的线性阶近似下)。首先针对静态赤道源建立自旋参数与高阶图像角位移的直接关系,然后将构造扩展到圆形赤道轨道上的紧凑源,通过在相应的延迟源位置评估每个图像贡献来处理源运动效应。

Result: 对于静态赤道源,获得了无量纲Kerr自旋参数与同一宇称序列中连续高阶图像角位移之间的直接关系。对于轨道运动源,发现参考系拖曳效应和源运动会修改图像的角位置,并在长基线可见度模式中留下独特印记。这两种效应在相反的角方向上作用,最终结构由它们之间的竞争决定。

Conclusion: 建立了用于识别黑洞自旋和源动力学在高阶图像干涉测量特征中印记的解析框架。该框架为利用长基线干涉测量从高阶图像中提取黑洞时空几何信息和源运动特性提供了理论基础,有助于未来黑洞观测数据的精确解读。

Abstract: Higher-order images produced by photons that orbit close to the unstable photon region of a black hole encode information about the spacetime geometry and leave characteristic signatures accessible with long interferometric baselines. We develop an analytical description of these signatures for a compact source in the spacetime of a slowly rotating Kerr black hole. Using the strong deflection limit, we derive the complex visibility function for a source of finite size at linear order in the black-hole spin. For a static equatorial source, we obtain a direct relation between the dimensionless Kerr spin parameter and the angular displacement of successive higher-order images belonging to the same parity sequence. We then extend the construction to a compact source on a circular equatorial orbit by evaluating each image contribution at the corresponding retarded source position. Frame dragging and source motion modify the angular positions of the images and leave distinct imprints on the long-baseline visibility pattern. For the configuration considered here, these effects act in opposite angular directions, so that the resulting structure is determined by their competition. Our results provide an analytical framework for identifying the imprints of black-hole spin and source dynamics in the interferometric signatures of higher-order images.

</details>


### [129] [Note on decoherence of the cosmological background by thermal perturbations in the language of open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11397)
*Alexander Kulyabin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在玩具模型中研究宇宙学扰动的热浴如何使宇宙的均匀自由度退相干，将扰动视为开放量子系统框架中的环境。通过有效二阶耦合纳入宇宙学反作用并推导主方程，发现热贡献对应的特征退相干时间尺度可短于哈勃时间尺度，并探讨了退相干诱导的指针态结构能否为早期宇宙半经典态的选择提供判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期研究通过从总波函数的半经典近似中追踪扰动来处理退相干问题，但这种方法存在局限性。本文旨在采用更完善的开放量子系统框架，通过有效耦合纳入宇宙学扰动的反作用效应，更准确地描述宇宙均匀模式的量子退相干过程，并探索退相干机制能否为早期宇宙半经典态的选择提供物理依据。

Method: 构建包含空间平坦半经典暴胀宇宙和慢滚时钟场的玩具模型，将处于类热态的标量宇宙学扰动作为环境。通过有效二阶耦合项纳入宇宙学反作用，推导出描述系统演化的主方程（master equation）。模型的关键特征是不使用马尔可夫近似，耦合常数由扰动模式的热关联函数确定。通过分析主方程研究退相干时间尺度和指针态结构。

Result: 对于代表性的参数估计，热贡献对应的特征退相干时间尺度可以短于哈勃时间尺度，表明宇宙学扰动的热浴能够在宇宙学相关的时间内有效地使均匀模式退相干。此外，研究发现退相干诱导的指针态结构可能为早期宇宙中半经典态的选择提供物理判据。

Conclusion: 本文通过开放量子系统框架成功建立了宇宙学扰动热浴导致宇宙均匀自由度退相干的理论模型。研究表明，在不依赖马尔可夫近似的情况下，通过二阶有效耦合和热关联函数可以准确描述退相干过程。退相干时间尺度可与哈勃时间尺度相比拟甚至更短，证明了该机制的宇宙学相关性。退相干框架中的指针态结构为早期宇宙半经典态的选择提供了新的物理视角，对理解宇宙早期量子-经典过渡具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study, within a toy model, how a thermal bath of cosmological perturbations can decohere the homogeneous degrees of freedom of the universe, treating the perturbations as an environment in the framework of open quantum systems. In contrast to several earlier approaches, instead of tracing out the perturbations from a semiclassical ansatz for the total wave function, we incorporate their cosmological backreaction through an effective second-order coupling and derive a master equation. Our toy model consists of a spatially flat semiclassical inflationary universe with a clock field in the slow-roll regime, while scalar cosmological perturbations in an assumed thermal-like state constitute the environment. One of the features of the model is the absence of the Markov approximation. The resulting coupling constant is determined by thermal correlation functions of the quantized perturbation modes. Within this framework, we find that, for a representative set of parameter estimates, the characteristic decoherence timescale corresponding to the thermal contribution can be shorter than the Hubble timescale. In addition, we address the question of whether the pointer-state structure induced by the decoherence in such a framework could provide a criterion for the choice of semiclassical states in the early universe.

</details>


### [130] [Bulk-boundary decompositions and exact perfect fluid stellar distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11518)
*Sudan Hansraj,Christian G. Boehmer,Ndumiso Buthelezi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Böhmer和Jensko提出的引力场论中Ricci标量分解理论,针对恒星完美流体分布在conformostatic度规背景下的场方程进行建模,重点分析了体项的纯二次形式情况,构建了满足物理要求的各向同性流体壳层模型和等温流体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Einstein引力理论的线性形式在处理复杂天体物理建模时存在局限性,需要探索更高复杂度的引力场论。Böhmer-Jensko理论通过将Ricci标量分解为体项和边界项提供了新的理论框架,其中体项的函数形式选择对天体物理建模至关重要,因此有必要研究超越线性情况的纯二次形式。

Method: 1) 采用conformostatic度规作为背景时空,推导恒星完美流体分布的场方程;2) 针对体项函数的纯二次形式建立场方程系统;3) 分析压力各向同性条件下的方程,尝试几何势函数的简单选择;4) 研究势函数与半径成反比的情况;5) 假设势函数之间存在正比关系以寻找可行解;6) 构建各向同性流体壳层模型并验证物理合理性;7) 研究密度和压力都遵循半径平方反比律的等温流体情况。

Result: 在几何势函数与半径成反比的情况下未能得到物理上可行的模型。通过假设势函数之间的正比关系,成功构建了满足基本物理要求的各向同性流体壳层模型。对于等温流体模型(密度和压力均服从半径平方反比律),获得了与相应Einstein模型中流体行为相当的结果。纯二次情况下的场方程比Einstein方程复杂得多,压力各向同性方程即使在简单的几何势函数选择下也难以处理。

Conclusion: Böhmer-Jensko引力理论的纯二次形式为天体物理建模提供了可行的替代方案。虽然场方程的复杂性显著增加,但通过适当的势函数关系假设,可以构建物理上合理的各向同性流体模型。特别是等温流体模型展现出与Einstein理论相当的流体行为,表明该修改引力理论在特定条件下能够产生与广义相对论一致的物理预测,为进一步探索修改引力理论在恒星结构建模中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: The decomposition of the Ricci scalar in terms of bulk and boundary terms gives rise to an interesting gravitational field theory as proposed by Böhmer and Jensko (BJ). BJ went on to suggest functional forms of the bulk contributions in the Lagrangian. For the purposes of astrophysical modelling the boundary terms are non-dynamical and the entire theory is built from the bulk contribution only. The field equations for a stellar perfect fluid distribution are obtained against the background of a conformostatic metric. Since the linear form of the function of the bulk term is equivalent to Einstein we probe the next level of complexity namely the pure quadratic case. The field equations are substantially more complicated than Einstein's equations. The equation of pressure isotropy proves intractable even with simple choices of one of the geometric potential functions. Accordingly we analyse the case of the potentials varying inversely with the radius. Viable models do not arise in this case and so we postulate a proportional relationship between the potentials. It turns out that a physically reasonable shell of isotropic fluid may be constructed that satisfies elementary physical requirements. Finally we investigate the isothermal fluid where both density and pressure go as the inverse square law of the radius. This results in fluid behaviour that is comparable to that in the corresponding Einstein model.

</details>


### [131] [Holographic dark energy from generalized entropies and extended infrared cut-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11616)
*Ke Chen,Simone D'Onofrio,Sergei D. Odintsov,Tanmoy Paul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于广义熵泛函和局域红外截断构建的全息暗能量模型，将三种熵（Barrow熵、三参数和五参数广义熵）分别与Granda-Oliveros截断及其两种扩展相结合，通过DESI、Pantheon+和Planck数据进行MCMC约束分析，发现数据支持高阶红外截断但未显示偏离Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律的显著证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管全息暗能量模型已被广泛研究，但不同广义熵泛函与红外截断组合的系统性比较仍然缺乏。特别是需要探究：(1)不同熵形式（Barrow熵和多参数广义熵）如何影响暗能量演化；(2)红外截断中加入高阶项（如H^{-2}或二阶导数项）能否改善与观测数据的拟合；(3)这些模型对哈勃常数的预测及对Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律可能偏离的约束程度。

Method: 构建了九个全息暗能量模型，通过将三种熵（Barrow熵S_B、三参数广义熵S_3、五参数广义熵S_5）分别与三种红外截断（标准Granda-Oliveros截断、添加γH^{-2}项的扩展、添加γ(Ḧ/H)项的二阶扩展）组合。使用MCMC方法对模型进行参数约束，数据集包括DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+超新星样本和Planck压缩CMB似然。采用Akaike信息准则(AIC)和贝叶斯信息准则(BIC)对模型进行比较和选择，并分析了暗能量状态方程的演化行为。

Result: 所有模型中哈勃常数保持稳定。H^{-2}项在所有情况下都未被约束，因其仅在遥远未来才变得重要，且从未改善GO截断的拟合。Ḧ/H项对Barrow熵和S_5受到严格约束并显著改善拟合，但对S_3无改善。最佳拟合由带Ḧ/H截断的Barrow熵模型获得，但Barrow指数在所有情况下仍与零相容。S_3和S_5的暗能量状态方程显示类精质行为和穿越幽灵分界线的现象。

Conclusion: 观测数据倾向于支持高阶（二阶导数）红外截断形式，但未显示偏离Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律的显著证据，因为Barrow指数在统计上与零相容。这表明虽然动力学修正（通过高阶截断项）能改善模型与数据的符合度，但熵的修正形式（偏离标准面积定律）在当前观测精度下尚未得到强有力支持。研究还揭示了某些广义熵模型可能展现丰富的暗能量状态方程演化特征。

Abstract: We study holographic dark energy models constructed from generalized entropy functionals and local infrared cut-offs. Three entropies (Barrow and the three and five-parameter generalized entropies) are each combined with the Granda-Oliveros (GO) cut-off and two extensions, one adding a $γH^{-2}$ term and one a $γ\ddot{H}/H$ term, the latter raising the dark-energy evolution equation to second order. The nine resulting models are constrained against DESI DR2 BAO, Pantheon+ and a Planck compressed CMB likelihood through an MCMC analysis, and compared using the Akaike and Bayesian information criteria. The Hubble constant is stable across all models. The $H^{-2}$ term is unconstrained in every case, as it becomes relevant only in the far future, and never improves on the GO cut-off. The $\ddot{H}/H$ term, instead, is tightly constrained for Barrow and $S_5$, where it improves the fit substantially, but not for $S_3$. The best fit is obtained for Barrow entropy with the $\ddot{H}/H$ cut-off, yet the Barrow exponent remains compatible with zero in all cases. The data thus favour a higher-order infrared cut-off, while showing no significant evidence for a departure from the Bekenstein-Hawking area law. We also investigate the dark energy equation of state (EoS) of $S_3$ and $S_5$, discovering quintessence-like behavior and phantom crossing.

</details>


### [132] [FLRW-Compatible Tensors and Perfect-Fluid Universality in Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11626)
*Cem Dalkırmaz,Yaghoub Heydarzade*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了(D+1)维FLRW时空上与空间均匀性和各向同性群不变的协变张量场,证明了当D>N时,任意N阶张量场可以分解为由度规张量和类时单位矢量构成的单项式的有限和,系数仅依赖于宇宙时间。该结果为修正引力理论中FLRW背景上的完美流体结构提供了任意阶张量分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力理论中,FLRW背景场方程的修正项往往表现出完美流体的形式结构。然而,此前缺乏对这种普适性现象的深层数学理解,特别是对于任意阶张量场的系统分类。本文旨在从张量分析的角度,为修正引力方程在FLRW时空背景下呈现完美流体结构提供严格的数学基础和任意阶张量的普适性证明。

Method: 采用张量分解的数学方法,研究配备Levi-Civita联络的(D+1)维FLRW时空上的张量场。核心方法是证明在空间均匀性和各向同性群作用下保持不变的N阶协变张量场(D>N时),可以有限分解为由度规张量g_μν和类时单位矢量u_μ构成的单项式,其标量系数仅依赖于宇宙时间。通过这种分解方法,系统地分析了张量场在对称性约束下的结构特征。

Result: 证明了当D>N时,任意满足空间均匀性和各向同性的N阶协变张量场都可以表示为有限个由度规张量和类时矢量构成的单项式之和。作为直接推论,对于D>2的情况,FLRW背景场方程类爱因斯坦重排中出现的任意二阶修正项都具有有效完美流体的形式。该结果补充了现有的二阶张量普适性结果,并将其推广到任意阶张量情况。文中还讨论了该结果在广义Friedmann方程和修正引力中测地偏离方程中的应用。

Conclusion: 本文建立了FLRW时空上张量场在对称性约束下的完整分类框架,从数学上严格证明了修正引力理论中完美流体结构的张量起源。该结果表明,FLRW背景下修正引力方程的完美流体形式不是偶然现象,而是源于时空对称性对张量场结构的根本约束。这一任意阶张量的普适性结果为理解和构造修正引力理论提供了重要的理论工具,对广义Friedmann方程和测地偏离方程等具体物理问题具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: We study FLRW-compatible tensor fields on Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker spacetimes in $(D+1)$ dimensions equipped with Levi-Civita connection. We prove that any covariant rank-$N$ tensor field invariant under the spatial homogeneity and isotropy group, with $D>N$, admits a finite decomposition into monomials constructed from the metric tensor $g_{μν}$ and the unit timelike vector $u_μ$, with scalar coefficients depending only on cosmic time. As a direct consequence, any rank-two correction term appearing in an Einstein-like rearrangement of the FLRW background field equations has the effective perfect-fluid form for $D>2$. Thus, the result provides an arbitrary-rank tensorial classification underlying the perfect-fluid structure of modified gravity equations on FLRW backgrounds, complementing existing rank-two universality results. Applications to generalized Friedmann equations and to the geodesic deviation equation in modified gravity are also discussed.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond the Dolgov-Kawasaki instability: Covariant scalaron stability, entropy transport, and inner horizons in f(R) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11649)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Francisco Tello-Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了度规f(R)引力中Dolgov-Kawasaki不稳定性与强场视界动力学的分离问题,通过Legendre势和精确球对称Wald面积输运恒等式,证明了在固定常曲率背景下输运仅依赖于(f,f_R),而scalaron质量可独立调节,为耦合正则性、背景输运、scalaron稳定性和非线性内视界演化提供了分层框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dolgov-Kawasaki不稳定性只能测试f(R)引力中局部scalaron响应,无法确定强场视界动力学行为。需要将这两个问题分离开来研究,以理解背景输运性质与scalaron稳定性之间的独立关系,特别是在黑洞视界附近的物理行为。

Method: 引入Legendre变量F=f_R和Legendre势U(F)=FR-f来重新表述理论;推导精确球对称Wald面积输运恒等式,该恒等式抵消scalaron Hessian项;在Einstein框架下区分纵向动能聚焦和依赖势的交叉聚焦;使用f(R)=R+αR²中的Reissner-Nordström解作为精确判别器;通过变化F的带电解和宇宙学检验来测试恒等式在常数F扇区之外的有效性;对传输到非退化Cauchy视界的大质量scalaron尾部进行固定背景估计。

Result: 证明了在固定常曲率背景和无迹物质条件下,输运仅依赖于(f,f_R),而scalaron质量m_sc²=(F-Rf_RR)/(3f_RR)依赖于可独立调节的f_RR;在f(R)=R+αR²的Reissner-Nordström解中,相同的背景输运可与相反符号的scalaron质量共存;对于传输到非退化Cauchy视界的非零大质量scalaron尾部,条件固定背景估计给出广义熵聚焦的非可积贡献;该估计未确立非线性质量膨胀或特定的可延拓性类别。

Conclusion: 研究为f(R)引力中的耦合正则性、背景输运、scalaron稳定性和非线性内视界演化提供了分层框架。通过精确的输运恒等式和Einstein框架解释,成功将背景几何输运性质与scalaron局部稳定性分离,表明这两者可以独立调控。Reissner-Nordström精确解证明了相同输运背景下scalaron质量符号可反转的判别性质,为理解f(R)引力中的强场动力学和视界结构提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The Dolgov-Kawasaki instability tests the local scalaron response in metric $f(R)$ gravity, but does not determine strong-field horizon dynamics. We separate these questions using $F=f_R$ and the Legendre potential $\mathcal U(F)=FR-f$. The scalaron restoring coefficient is $m_{\rm sc}^2=(F-Rf_{RR})/(3f_{RR})$, whereas exact spherical Wald-area transport identities cancel the scalaron Hessian. Their Einstein-frame interpretation distinguishes longitudinal kinetic focusing from potential-dependent cross-focusing. We prove that, at fixed constant-curvature background with tracefree matter, transport depends only on $(f,f_R)$, while the scalaron mass depends on the independently adjustable $f_{RR}$. Reissner-Nordström in $f(R)=R+αR^2$ gives an exact discriminator: identical background transport coexists with opposite scalaron mass signs. A charged solution with varying $F$ and a cosmological check test the identities beyond this constant-$F$ sector. For a nonzero massive scalaron tail transmitted to a nondegenerate Cauchy horizon, a conditional fixed-background estimate gives a nonintegrable contribution to generalized entropy focusing. This estimate does not establish nonlinear mass inflation or a particular extendibility class. The results provide a layered framework for coupling regularity, background transport, scalaron stability, and nonlinear inner-horizon evolution.

</details>


### [134] [Nonreciprocal black holes with primary hair in beyond Horndeski gravity: Exact solutions and stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11712)
*Sebastian Bahamonde,Theodoros Nakas,Simon Iteanu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统方法，用于构造超Horndeski理论中的精确静态渐近平坦黑洞解，突破了传统的倒易条件限制，发现了两类定性不同的毛发结构（khronometric毛发和真实标量毛发），并通过正则化获得了规则黑洞和孤子构型，线性扰动分析表明具有标量毛发的代表性解在所考虑的模式下是线性稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统构造超Horndeski理论中黑洞解的方法依赖于限制性的倒易条件g_{tt}g_{rr}=-1，这严重限制了可能的几何构型。为了探索更一般的黑洞解空间，需要发展一种不依赖该条件的系统性方法，允许两个独立的度规函数来刻画更广泛的几何结构，从而揭示理论中可能存在的新型黑洞毛发和物理性质。

Method: 发展了一种新的系统性构造方法，适用于具有平移对称性和Z_2对称性的超Horndeski理论，允许使用两个独立的度规函数描述几何。通过分析理论的不同分支，识别出两类定性不同的毛发：khronometric毛发和真实标量毛发。针对第二分支（真实标量毛发），推导了多个不同的精确黑洞解族。对于存在奇异性的解，采用正则化技术获得规则的黑洞或孤子构型。通过分析轴对称扰动和偶宇称扇区的单极(ℓ=0)模式，系统研究了这些解在线性扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 成功构造了超Horndeski理论中的多个精确静态渐近平坦黑洞解族，这些解具有两类定性不同的毛发结构。第一类是khronometric毛发，其平移对称性Noether流和相关传播自由度是平凡的；第二类是真实标量毛发，具有非零的Noether流和传播自由度。一般情况下，这些解是奇异的并展现出与独立标量荷相关的主标量毛发，但通过正则化可以得到规则黑洞或孤子构型，此时标量毛发变为次级的。对具有标量毛发的代表性解的扰动分析表明，在所考虑的模式（轴对称扰动和ℓ=0偶宇称模式）下，这些解是线性稳定的。

Conclusion: 本研究成功突破了传统倒易条件的限制，为超Horndeski理论中的黑洞解提供了更一般的系统性构造方法。发现了两类物理机制不同的毛发结构，丰富了该理论框架下黑洞解的多样性。通过正则化技术获得的规则黑洞和孤子构型，以及线性稳定性分析的正面证据，表明这些新解可能具有物理可实现性。该方法为探索修改引力理论中更复杂的黑洞结构开辟了新途径，对理解标量-张量理论中的黑洞物理具有重要意义。

Abstract: We develop a novel systematic method for constructing exact, static, and asymptotically flat black-hole solutions in shift-symmetric and $Z_2$-symmetric beyond Horndeski theories. In contrast to previous studies, our approach does not rely on the restrictive reciprocal condition $g_{tt}g_{rr}=-1$, allowing for more general geometries characterized by two independent metric functions. The resulting solution space contains two qualitatively distinct types of hair originating from different branches of the theory. One branch is characterized by khronometric hair, while the other by genuine scalar hair. In both, the scalar profile defines a nontrivial preferred foliation, but the shift-symmetry Noether current and the associated propagating degree of freedom are trivial for the former and nonvanishing for the latter. Focusing mainly on the second branch, we derive several distinct families of exact black-hole solutions. In general, these solutions are singular and exhibit primary scalar hair associated with an independent scalar charge. Nevertheless, we demonstrate that they can be regularized to yield regular black-hole or solitonic configurations, for which the scalar hair becomes secondary. We further investigate the stability properties of these solutions under linear perturbations by analyzing the axial sector and the monopole $(\ell=0)$ mode of the even-parity sector. For representative solutions with scalar hair, the perturbative analysis provides evidence for linear stability under the modes considered.

</details>


### [135] [Testing Extra-Dimensional Gravitational-Wave Polarizations with Compact-Binary Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11855)
*Hai-Tian Wang,Dicong Liang,Lijing Shao,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用引力波探测额外空间维度，通过对致密双星并合事件的贝叶斯分析，约束了额外维度投影到可观测三维空间所引入的旋转角参数，结果显示广义相对论预测在所有测试案例中均落在90%置信区域内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 额外空间维度是超越四维广义相对论理论中的自然产物，但缺乏直接观测证据。引力波提供了独特的探测手段：额外维度的几何投影可能在可观测的三维空间中产生张量、矢量和标量极化分量的混合效应，为验证额外维度存在提供了新途径。

Method: 研究采用了以下方法：(1)考虑一个额外空间维度，建立双模张量波理论框架；(2)将投影后的极化分量表示为两个基础广义相对论张量波形的线性组合；(3)通过标准探测器天线响应函数分析应变信号；(4)对五个致密双星并合事件进行频域贝叶斯分析；(5)约束两种非对易旋转排序下保持波在可观测子空间传播的两个旋转角；(6)对部分事件进一步分析允许波传播倾斜进入额外维度的四种排序（三角度拟合）。

Result: 分析结果显示：对于GW250114事件，在测试的旋转排序中，双角度拟合的广义相对论最大分位数为Q_GR≈0.6%，三角度拟合在对第三个角度边缘化后为Q_GR^marg≈4.6%。在所有五个分析事件中，广义相对论参考点均落在估计的90%最高后验密度区域内，表明观测数据与标准广义相对论预测相符。

Conclusion: 引力波观测数据对额外空间维度模型提供了有效约束。尽管理论上额外维度会通过几何投影产生非标准极化效应，但当前五个致密双星事件的分析结果均与四维广义相对论预测一致，广义相对论在所有测试配置下均未被排除。这为额外维度的存在性设定了观测限制，同时展示了引力波作为探测超越标准理论新物理的强大工具。

Abstract: Extra spatial dimensions arise naturally in theories beyond four-dimensional general relativity (GR), but direct observational evidence remains elusive. Gravitational waves offer a distinctive probe: geometric projection onto the observable three-dimensional spatial subspace can produce apparent tensor, vector, and scalar polarizations. We consider one additional spatial dimension and a two-mode tensor-wave ansatz for which the projected polarization components are fixed linear combinations of the same two underlying GR tensor waveforms. These components enter the measured strain through the standard detector antenna patterns. A frequency-domain Bayesian analysis of five compact-binary events constrains two rotation angles for two noncommuting orderings of rotations that preserve propagation within the observed subspace. Two events are further analyzed for four orderings that allow propagation to tilt into the extra dimension. For GW250114, the maximum GR quantiles among the tested rotation orderings are $Q_{\mathrm{GR}}\simeq0.6\%$ for the two-angle fits and $Q^{\mathrm{marg}}_{\mathrm{GR}}\simeq4.6\%$ for the three-angle fits after marginalizing over the third angle. Across the full event sample, the GR reference point lies inside the estimated 90% highest-posterior-density region in every case.

</details>


### [136] [Sharing of Gaussian tripartite steering in de-Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11874)
*Xin-Ze Song,Xiao-Li Huang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了de-Sitter时空中高斯三方量子操控的再分配和方向性特性，发现de-Sitter曲率会抑制预存在的量子操控但表现出强烈的方向依赖性，并能在因果不连通区域间产生和重新分配方向性多体量子操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索de-Sitter时空曲率对多体量子关联（特别是三方量子操控）的影响机制，理解引力场如何在宇宙学视界分隔的因果不连通区域间重新分配和生成方向性量子关联，这对于理解量子信息在弯曲时空中的行为具有重要意义。

Method: 采用连续变量框架，通过Bogoliubov变换将Bunch-Davies真空用开图模式表示，推导出多体高斯态的协方差矩阵，然后分析一对二和二对一两种量子操控配置，定量研究de-Sitter曲率参数对不同方向操控强度的影响，识别突然死亡和突然诞生等转变现象。

Result: 发现de-Sitter曲率普遍抑制可访问模式间的初始量子操控，但影响强烈依赖于操控方向；部分操控配置在有限曲率下出现突然死亡，而向非引力模式的二对一操控在整个参数范围保持有限值，展现出三方操控相对二方操控的鲁棒性；最大操控不对称性出现在一个方向操控突然死亡的临界点，标志着从双向到单向操控的转变；de-Sitter引力场能在宇宙学视界分隔的模式间产生三方操控，根据操控分区呈现持续单向、不对称双向或从单向到双向突然诞生等行为模式。

Conclusion: de-Sitter时空曲率不仅降解预存在的量子关联，还能在因果不连通区域间重新分配和生成具有方向性的多体量子操控。三方量子操控在弯曲时空中表现出比二方操控更丰富的动力学行为和更强的鲁棒性，曲率诱导的操控展现出复杂的方向依赖性和相变特征，这为理解量子信息在引力场中的行为提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We investigate the redistribution and directional properties of Gaussian tripartite quantum steering in de-Sitter space within the continuous-variable framework. By expressing the Bunch-Davies vacuum in terms of the open-chart modes through a Bogoliubov transformation, we derive the covariance matrix of the resulting multipartite Gaussian state and analyze both one-to-two and two-to-one steering configurations. We find that de-Sitter curvature generally suppresses the steering initially shared among the accessible modes, but its influence depends strongly on the steering direction. In particular, while some steering configurations undergo sudden death at finite curvature, two-to-one steering toward a nongravitational mode remains finite throughout the parameter range considered, demonstrating a robustness of tripartite steering absent in its bipartite counterpart. Remarkably, the maximum steering asymmetry occurs at the critical point at which steering in one direction undergoes sudden death, marking the transition from two-way to one-way steering. We further show that the de-Sitter gravitational field can generate tripartite steering involving modes separated by the cosmological horizon. Depending on the steering partition, this curvature-induced steering exhibits persistent one-way or asymmetric two-way behavior, as well as a sudden-birth transition from one-way to two-way steering. These results demonstrate that de-Sitter curvature not only degrades preexisting quantum correlations but also redistributes and generates directional multipartite steering across causally disconnected regions.

</details>


### [137] [Testing General Relativity with Binary Lens Microlensing Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11949)
*Xinyang Jin,Shuo Cao,Xuanni Chen,Yalong Nan,Xuchen Tai,Shuxun Tian*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出利用双星微引力透镜事件结合干涉天文测量技术，通过比较引力透镜质量与动力学质量来测量PPN参数γ，从而在极弱引力场(Φ~10^-8)regime下检验广义相对论，基于ASASSN-22av事件的模拟达到约23%的精度，未来可提升至3%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论虽然在星系和太阳系尺度上得到精确验证，但在极弱引力场(无量纲牛顿势Φ~10^-8)下的约束仍然很弱。传统双星透镜的天文测量受限于目标暗弱和极小角分离(典型几毫角秒)，需要新方法和新仪器来探测这一regime并测量单位质量产生的空间曲率

Method: 提出利用双星微引力透镜事件作为实验室，结合两种质量测量方法：(1)从微引力透镜效应推断的透镜质量M_lens^GR；(2)从干涉天文测量确定的动力学质量M_dyn。通过比较这两个质量测量值来确定参数化后牛顿(PPN)参数γ。采用GRAVITY+仪器克服灵敏度和测量精度限制，并对双星微引力透镜事件ASASSN-22av进行天文测量模拟

Result: 基于ASASSN-22av事件的天文测量模拟，直接估算PPN参数γ的精度达到约23%。研究表明，通过更多具有精确动力学/透镜质量测定的双星微引力透镜事件，这一测试精度可以进一步提升至约3%

Conclusion: 本研究成功展示了在极弱引力场regime下检验广义相对论的新方法。双星微引力透镜事件结合GRAVITY+等先进干涉天文测量技术，可以有效测量PPN参数γ，为在Φ~10^-8量级的引力场中检验引力理论提供了可行途径，未来有望将测量精度显著提升

Abstract: Einstein's General Relativity (GR), which has been precisely tested on galaxy and solar system scales, is still poorly constrained in extremely weak gravitational fields (with dimensionless Newtonian potentials of $Φ\sim 10^{-8}$). In this Letter, we propose a new method to probe such a weak-field regime of gravity and measure the spatial curvature generated per unit mass. Specifically, binary microlensing events provide a laboratory to measure the parameterized post-Newtonian (PPN) parameter ($γ$), by combining the lensing mass ($M_{\rm lens}^{\rm GR}$) inferred from microlensing with the dynamical mass ($M_{\rm dyn}$) determined from interferometric astrometry. Astrometric tracking of binary lenses was previously hindered by their faintness and minuscule angular separations (typically a few milliarcseconds). This limitation has been alleviated by the deployment of GRAVITY+, which pushes the instrument's sensitivity and astrometric precision to unprecedented heights. Based on astrometric simulations of the binary microlensing event ASASSN-22av, we directly estimate $γ$ at the precision of ~23%. Such test of GR in the extremely weak-field regime can be further improved to much higher precision (~3%), based on more binary microlensing events with precise determination of the dynamical/lens mass.

</details>


### [138] [Gravitational radiation from a photon on the light ring: multipole fluxes and the logarithmic divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12082)
*Enrico Barausse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Schwarzschild和Kerr黑洞光子圆轨道上的无质量粒子的引力波辐射特性,发现其辐射功率按1/ℓ衰减导致总功率对数发散,并推导出解析表达式,还分析了大质量粒子在光环附近轨道的情况以及奇偶宇称项的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的黑洞引力波辐射研究主要关注稳定轨道上的粒子,但对于位于不稳定的圆形零测地线(光环)上的无质量粒子的辐射特性缺乏系统的解析研究。特别是其多极辐射功率的渐近行为、总功率的收敛性以及辐射在视界和无穷远处的分配规律尚不清楚,需要通过Zerilli-Regge-Wheeler方程和Teukolsky方程进行严格的解析推导和数值验证。

Method: 采用Zerilli-Regge-Wheeler方程和Teukolsky方程作为理论框架,以零应力能量张量作为源项,对Schwarzschild和Kerr黑洞时空中光子圆轨道上的无质量粒子进行解析计算。推导各多极ℓ的辐射功率表达式,分析其渐近行为的物理机制(准正规模式近共振和无质量粒子源项的特殊抵消效应)。通过数值求解验证解析结果(Schwarzschild情况计算至ℓ=12800,Kerr情况至ℓ=800)。将分析推广到光环附近的大质量粒子轨道,并与历史文献中的测地同步辐射公式进行对比。

Result: 辐射功率在所有多极ℓ上按1/ℓ衰减,具体表达式为Ėℓ→g(a)κE²/(M²ℓ),其中κ=0.063653,自旋因子g(a)的闭合形式为g=27M²(r₀-M)/[r₀²(r₀+3M)],r₀为Kerr光环半径。1/ℓ行为源于两个原因:光子与准正规模式的近共振(阻止大ℓ时的指数抑制)和无质量粒子源项中的特殊抵消(使功率相比一般源降低ℓ²因子)。主导阶近似下一半辐射落入黑洞,一半逃逸至无穷远,并计算了非旋转情况下对这一均分的首阶修正。对于光环附近的大质量粒子,质量效应使1/ℓ律转变为指数截断。重新得到了Breuer等人(1973)大质量粒子测地同步辐射公式的主导偶宇称项,但发现其奇宇称项偏小4倍。所有解析结果通过高阶数值解得到验证。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统地解析证明了黑洞光环上无质量粒子的引力波辐射功率具有1/ℓ的多极衰减规律,导致总瞬时功率对数发散,这一反常行为源于准正规模式共振和源项抵消的双重效应。建立了适用于任意自旋Kerr黑洞的精确解析公式,揭示了辐射在视界吸收和无穷远逃逸之间的分配规律,并修正了历史文献中关于大质量粒子奇宇称辐射项的错误。该研究加深了对极端轨道引力波辐射机制的理解,为引力波天文学和黑洞物理提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: A massless particle on the circular null geodesic of a Schwarzschild or Kerr black hole radiates gravitational waves at all multipoles $\ell$ with a power that falls off only as $1/\ell$, so that the total instantaneous power is logarithmically divergent. We derive this result analytically from the Zerilli--Regge--Wheeler and Teukolsky equations with a null stress-energy tensor as source, and find that both the power radiated to infinity and the power absorbed by the horizon approach $\dot{E}_\ell\to g(a) κE^2/(M^2\ell)$, where $E$ is the energy of the particle, $M$ the mass of the black hole and $a$ its spin, with $κ=0.063653$ and a spin factor $g(a)$, for which we obtain, for any spin, the closed form $g=27M^2(r_0-M)/[r_0^2(r_0+3M)]$ in terms of the radius $r_0$ of the Kerr light ring. The $1/\ell$ behavior has two causes: the near-resonance of the photon with the quasinormal modes, which prevents any exponential suppression at large $\ell$, and a cancellation in the source that is specific to massless particles, which reduces the power per multipole by a factor $\ell^2$ with respect to a generic source. At leading order half of the radiation falls into the black hole, and we compute the first correction to this equal split in the non-spinning case. The same analysis describes massive particles on orbits close to the light ring, where the mass turns the $1/\ell$ law into an exponential cut-off. We recover the dominant, even-parity term of the geodesic synchrotron radiation formulae for massive particles of Breuer, Ruffini, Tiomno and Vishveshwara (1973), but we find that their odd-parity term is too small by a factor of four. All results are checked against numerical solutions, up to $\ell=12800$ in Schwarzschild and $\ell=800$ in Kerr.

</details>


### [139] [All theories of gravity with second-order traced field equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12097)
*Ángel J. Murcia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对具有二阶导数迹场方程的微分同胚不变引力理论进行了完整分类，证明了关于由黎曼张量代数构造的理论形式的猜想，并分类了所有具有二阶迹场方程的多项式准拓扑引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论虽然成功，但在量子化和宇宙学常数问题等方面存在困难。研究具有二阶场方程的修正引力理论可以避免Ostrogradsky不稳定性，同时保持微分同胚不变性。需要系统分类满足这些条件的所有可能引力理论，以理解引力理论的完整理论空间。

Method: 采用数学分类方法，系统研究满足以下条件的引力理论：(1) 微分同胚不变性；(2) 场方程的迹在导数上为二阶。通过代数方法从黎曼张量出发构造理论，使用张量分析和微分几何工具进行严格证明。特别关注多项式准拓扑引力理论的分类，验证并证明相关理论猜想。

Result: 完成了具有二阶迹场方程的微分同胚不变引力理论的完整分类。肯定性地证明了关于从黎曼张量代数构造的具有二阶迹场方程理论形式的猜想。给出了所有满足二阶迹场方程条件的多项式准拓扑引力理论的完整分类列表。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了具有二阶导数迹场方程的微分同胚不变引力理论的完整理论框架和分类体系。证明的猜想为理解黎曼张量代数构造的引力理论提供了理论基础。多项式准拓扑引力的分类结果界定了这类修正引力理论的完整理论空间，为进一步研究避免高阶导数病态的引力理论提供了系统的理论指导。

Abstract: We classify all diffeomorphism-invariant theories of gravity which possess traced field equations of motion of second order in derivatives. We also prove in the affirmative a conjecture regarding the form of theories constructed algebraically from the Riemann tensor with second-order traced field equations and classify all polynomial quasi-topological gravities with second-order traced field equations.

</details>


### [140] [The variance of the CMB temperature gradient: a dependence on the spatial orientation and anisotropy of a multiply connected Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12179)
*Ralf Aurich,Martin J France,Frank Steiner*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种模型无关的方法来揭示宇宙微波背景(CMB)温度图中的各向异性和几何模式,通过ρ统计量和掩模扫描过程,证实了3-环面拓扑宇宙中CMB各向异性的几何周期结构,并确定宇宙有限尺度约为3个哈勃长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙微波背景辐射中存在微弱但持续的几何和周期性结构,需要一种模型无关的方法来揭示这些各向异性模式,以验证宇宙可能具有有限尺度的3-环面拓扑结构,并探测"天空中的圆圈"等预期几何特征。

Method: 采用基于ρ统计量(CMB温度梯度归一化方差的标准差)和掩模扫描过程的方法,在768个掩模方向上探索整个CMB。通过大量环面地图集合检测几何模式是否与预期的"天空中的圆圈"布局匹配,重新验证立方环面尺寸L与ρ之间的线性关系。

Result: 在环面拓扑地图上检测到的模式与预期的"天空中的圆圈"几何布局相匹配;重新验证了立方环面尺寸L与ρ统计量之间的线性关系;确定具有环面拓扑的宇宙有限尺度约为3个哈勃长度;发现统计各向同性的违反非常微弱,几乎不影响ρ和L之间的线性关系。

Conclusion: ρ统计量不仅是宇宙空间有限性和尺度的统计特征(无论其拓扑是单连通还是多连通),而且通过掩模扫描过程能够勾勒出CMB的各向异性模式。立方环面拓扑对宇宙施加周期性条件,约束了整体不均匀性程度并要求零平均曲率。该方法成功揭示了3-环面拓扑宇宙中CMB的微弱但持续的几何周期结构。

Abstract: In the present work we develop a model--independent method to reveal the underlying patterns of anisotropy and geometry in CMB temperature maps. This method, based on the rho-statistics and a process of mask--sweeping, unveils faint but persistent geometrical and periodic structures characterising the anisotropy of the CMB in a universe with 3-torus topology. We assess that the patterns detected over large ensembles of toroidal maps match geometrically to the layouts of expected circles-in-the-sky. The mask--sweeping method explores the whole CMB over 768 mask orientations. Thus we are able to re-verify the linear relation between L the size of the cubic torus and rho the standard deviation of the normalised variance of the CMB temperature gradient. This confers a finite size s of approximately 3 Hubble lengths to our Universe with toroidal topology. Furthermore, it is shown that the violation of statistical isotropy is so faint that this has almost no influence on the linear relation between rho and L. A topology like the cubic toroidal one imposes to our Universe periodic conditions (that can be seen as periodic boundary conditions) which constrain the global degree of inhomogeneity and a zero average curvature. We demonstrate here that rho is not only a statistical signature of the spatial finiteness and size of our Universe (may its topology be simply or multiply connected) but also outlines the patterns of anisotropy of the CMB with the help of the mask--sweeping process.

</details>


### [141] [Swirling spacetimes in higher dimensions: Vacuum backgrounds and black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12372)
*José Barrientos,Adolfo Cisterna,Carlos Herdeiro,Konstantinos Pallikaris*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用降维理论的隐藏对称性构造了真空广义相对论中的高维旋转时空，在五维中沿两个独立的轴向Killing矢量进行约化，获得了具有两个独立旋转参数的SL(3,ℝ)/SO(3) sigma模型和真正的五维背景，并揭示了新背景的多方向渐近结构和方向曲率奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高维旋转时空解通常只包含单一旋转参数，缺乏对多旋转参数时空的系统构造方法。利用降维理论的隐藏对称性和协变空间方法，可以构造出具有更丰富结构的高维旋转时空，特别是研究多方向旋转效应和新型渐近行为，探索广义相对论在高维情形下的新解及其性质。

Method: 采用协变空间形式化方法，在五维时空中沿两个独立的轴向Killing矢量进行维度约化，将问题降至三维并得到SL(3,ℝ)/SO(3) sigma模型。通过对该sigma模型进行变换生成新的五维解，并将相同变换应用于Schwarzschild-Tangherlini和Myers-Perry几何以获得双旋转版本。进一步分析新背景的渐近结构，通过关联的径向-角向极限研究方向曲率奇点，并考察类因果测地线的行为。最后系统研究该构造方法向更高维度的最大轴向扩展。

Result: 成功构造了具有两个独立旋转参数的真正五维背景，并获得了双旋转的Schwarzschild-Tangherlini和Myers-Perry几何。发现新背景具有多方向渐近结构，在关联的径向-角向极限下存在方向曲率奇点，这些奇点在通常的固定角度大半径极限下是不可见的。证明存在类因果测地线在有限仿射参数内到达这些奇异渐近端。确定了该构造的维度限制:五维是完全轴向约化至三维的最大维度,六维允许额外的非平凡平移形变,更高维度仅贡献平坦的旁观方向。

Conclusion: 本研究通过隐藏对称性和协变空间方法成功构造了高维多旋转时空解,揭示了广义相对论在高维情形下的新型渐近结构和奇点行为。新背景展现的方向曲率奇点和有限参数可达的奇异端点表明高维旋转时空具有比经典情形更丰富的几何结构。该构造方法的维度分析表明五维和六维具有特殊地位,为进一步研究高维引力理论的解空间提供了系统框架。这些结果对理解高维黑洞物理、全息对偶以及高维时空的因果结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: We construct higher-dimensional swirling spacetimes in vacuum General Relativity using the hidden symmetries of the dimensionally reduced theory. Working directly with the coset-space formulation, we perform in five dimensions a reduction along the two independent axial Killing vectors, obtaining an $SL(3,\mathbb{R})/SO(3)$ sigma model and a genuinely five-dimensional background with two independent swirling parameters. The same transformation yields doubly swirling Schwarzschild--Tangherlini and Myers--Perry geometries. A distinctive feature of the new background is its multidirectional asymptotic structure. Correlated radial-angular limits reveal directional curvature singularities that are invisible in the usual large-radius limit at fixed angle. We further exhibit causal geodesics that reach such singular asymptotic ends in finite affine parameter. Finally, we describe the maximally axial extension of this construction to higher dimensions: five dimensions are maximal for an entirely axial reduction to three dimensions, while six dimensions admit an additional nontrivial translational deformation, with further dimensions contributing only flat spectator directions.

</details>


### [142] [A Pairwise Surrogate for Gravitational-Wave Spectra from Highly Relativistic Vacuum Bubble Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12389)
*Malte Buschmann,Toby Opferkuch*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿真的成对替代方法，通过分离微观碰撞动力学和多气泡几何结构，解决了真空一阶相变引力波预测中高洛伦兹因子气泡碰撞的计算难题。该方法利用低维模拟构建双气泡引力波谱库，结合几何权重重构多气泡系统谱，相比直接晶格模拟可降低数个数量级的计算成本，并能研究高度相对论性碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接晶格模拟真空一阶相变中的引力波谱面临巨大挑战：需要在包含多个气泡的大体积中解析具有洛伦兹收缩的标量场气泡壁，特别是在大推进（高速碰撞）区域，计算成本极其高昂甚至无法实现。现有方法难以探索高度相对论性碰撞regime，限制了对引力波谱振幅和形状如何依赖于碰撞推进和标量势的研究。

Method: 开发了一种基于仿真的成对替代模型，核心思想是将微观碰撞动力学与多气泡转变几何结构解耦：(1) 利用对称性简化的(1+1)维标量场模拟构建双气泡引力波谱库，涵盖非线性碰撞后演化；(2) 引入几何权重来考虑其他气泡的屏蔽效应；(3) 将双气泡谱与几何权重结合重构多气泡系统的引力波谱；(4) 使用完整的(3+1)维晶格模拟验证双气泡谱以及三气泡和多气泡系统的重构谱。

Result: 替代模型通过了与完整(3+1)维晶格模拟的对比验证，能够准确重构三气泡和多气泡系统的引力波谱。计算成本相比直接多气泡模拟降低了数个数量级。该方法使得研究直接模拟实际无法达到的高度相对论性碰撞成为可能，能够系统探索引力波谱的振幅和形状如何依赖于碰撞推进因子和标量势参数。

Conclusion: 本文提出的成对替代方法为研究真空一阶相变引力波提供了高效可行的途径，通过分离尺度和利用低维模拟突破了传统晶格模拟在高推进区域的计算瓶颈。该方法不仅大幅降低计算成本，更重要的是拓展了可探索的参数空间至高度相对论性碰撞regime，为研究引力波谱特征与物理参数的依赖关系、进而用引力波观测约束早期宇宙相变物理提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from vacuum first-order phase transitions probe the nonlinear dynamics of relativistic bubble collisions. Predicting their spectrum requires resolving Lorentz-contracted scalar bubble walls throughout a volume containing many bubbles, making the large-boost regime prohibitively expensive for direct lattice simulations. We develop a simulation-based pairwise surrogate that separates the microscopic collision dynamics from the geometry of the multi-bubble transition. This utilizes symmetry-reduced $(1+1)$-dimensional scalar-field simulations to build a library of two-bubble gravitational-wave spectra, including the nonlinear post-collision evolution. These spectra are combined with geometric weights that account for screening by other bubbles. We test the two-bubble spectra and the reconstructed spectra of three- and many-bubble systems against full $(3+1)$-dimensional lattice simulations. Not only can the surrogate be orders of magnitude cheaper computationally, but it also provides access to highly relativistic collisions beyond the practical reach of direct many-bubble simulations. This provides a route to studying how the spectral amplitude and shape depend on the collision boost and scalar potential.

</details>


### [143] [Listening to the Horizon: Probing Near-Horizon Reflectivity with GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12429)
*R. Prasad,Subhodeep Sarkar,Sudipta Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用引力波事件GW250114中新识别的直接波(DW)成分,发展了一种检验黑洞视界反射性的新方法。通过搜索由视界表面反射产生的延迟回波信号,对奇异致密天体的表面反射率进行了限制,在90%置信度下得到反射率上限|ℛₛ|<0.06,将瞬发环降信号转化为视界性质的直接检验工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学的核心目标是检验天体物理黑洞的视界性质。新发现的直接波(DW)成分提供了探测近视界物理的新途径。虽然DW本身是否携带视界信息尚不明确,但研究者认为它可以用于构建检验视界反射性的新零假设检验方法。如果黑洞残余是具有部分反射表面的奇异致密天体(ECO),则内落的辐射会被反射并通过引力势垒泄漏回来,产生延迟的DW回波,其振幅由表面反射率决定。

Method: 研究团队构建了相应的回波模板,该模板以观测到的直接波(DW)进行归一化。在经过准正则模滤波的引力波数据中搜索这种回波信号。具体而言,针对距离拟视界从0.1倍最终质量Mf到普朗克长度范围内的反射表面,系统性地搜索由表面反射率|ℛₛ|决定的延迟回波特征。使用贝叶斯统计方法评估回波存在的证据强度。

Result: 在GW250114的数据分析中未发现回波信号的证据,贝叶斯因子为ln𝒷=-2.9(表明不支持回波存在的假设)。获得了表面反射率的90%置信度上限约束:|ℛₛ|<0.06,这对应于反射能量份额低于0.4%。该约束适用于距离拟视界从0.1Mf到普朗克长度尺度的反射表面。

Conclusion: 本研究成功地将引力波环降阶段的瞬发信号(直接波)转化为检验黑洞视界反射性的直接工具,建立了一种新的零假设检验方法。通过对GW250114事件的分析,对奇异致密天体表面反射率施加了严格的限制(反射率<6%,反射能量<0.4%),支持了天体物理黑洞具有吸收视界而非反射表面的标准图像。这一方法为未来利用引力波观测检验黑洞本质提供了新途径。

Abstract: Testing the horizon properties of astrophysical black holes is a central goal of gravitational-wave astronomy. The recently identified Direct Wave (DW), a prompt non-quasinormal component of GW250114, provides a new probe of near-horizon physics. While it remains unclear whether the DW itself carries horizon information, we show that it enables a novel null test of horizon reflectivity. If the remnant is an exotic compact object (ECO) with a partially reflecting surface, inward plunge radiation is reflected and leaks back through the gravitational potential barrier, producing a delayed DW echo whose amplitude is set by the surface reflectivity ($|\mathcal{R}_s|$). We construct the corresponding echo template, normalized by the observed DW, and search for it in quasinormal-mode-filtered data. We find no evidence for an echo ($\ln\mathcal{B}=-2.9$) and obtain the 90\% credible bound $|\mathcal{R}_s|<0.06$, corresponding to a reflected energy fraction below $0.4\%$, for surfaces from $0.1\, M_f$ to a Planck length from the would-be horizon. Our method thus turns the prompt ringdown signal into a direct test of horizon reflectivity.

</details>
